- **musts**: Use the `musts` CLI to validate that your changes are done. Run `musts validate` to get the validation todo list; for deterministic checks (cargo/*, bazel/build, bazel/test) run `musts run &lt;task-id&gt;` to let musts execute and record them for you; for judgment checks (agent, mav) do the work and record evidence with `musts evidence`; then re-run `musts validate` until it is empty. Use after any code change in a repo that has a `MUSTS.yml`. Also covers adding a `.mustsignore` (gitignore-style file) when local artefacts are making the validation loop noisier than it should be. ([bitomule/musts](https://github.com/bitomule/musts/tree/HEAD/skills/musts) · ★9)

Unlike the bomb skill (which works a whole target), bluff targets exactly one claim and stops at the verdict. ([yunst047/mutsuki-skills](https://github.com/yunst047/mutsuki-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/mutsuki-bluff))
- **mutsuki-bomb**: Throw the Love & Violence bag at one target and break it end to end: a function, a parser, an endpoint, a "done" feature. Use when the user says "try to break this", "stress test this", "find the edge cases in", "bomb this", "mutsuki-bomb", or invokes /mutsuki-bomb. Unlike the bluff skill (which falsifies one claim), bomb works a whole target until it breaks or honestly survives. - **mutsuki-dirt**: Mutsuki's intel-gathering trait — sweep a codebase for the buried gossip nobody talks about: swallowed exceptions, TODO/FIXME/HACK graveyards, hardcoded secrets, dead and commented-out code, skipped tests, unvalidated inputs, ignored return values, suspicious "temporary" workarounds.
